The `public contributions` by users in Uganda on `2026/11/11 4:29 AM UTC`. This list contains users from `Uganda` and cities `Kampala`.

There are `138 countries` and `674 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users).

There are `967 users`  in Uganda. You need at least `11 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Uganda GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/uganda.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
